<section class="hm-faq-section" id="hmFaq">
<div class="container">
<div class="hm-section-title"><span class="hm-eyebrow"><i class="fas fa-circle-question"></i> معلومات مهمة</span><h2>الأسئلة الشائعة</h2></div>
<div class="hm-faq-list">
<details><summary>واش الدفع عند الاستلام؟ <i class="fas fa-plus"></i></summary><p>نعم، كتخلصي الثمن عند الاستلام من بعد ما نتواصلوا معك لتأكيد المعلومات.</p></details>
<details><summary>كيفاش نختار المقاس؟ <i class="fas fa-plus"></i></summary><p>اختاري الموديل، وغادي يبانوا غير المقاسات المتوفرة. <button class="hm-faq-guide" data-guide-target="#hmSizes" type="button">ورّيني المقاسات</button></p></details>
<details><summary>كيفاش نختار اللون؟ <i class="fas fa-plus"></i></summary><p>الألوان كيبانو كدواير: فالهاتف اضغطي على اللون، وفالحاسوب مرري الماوس باش يبان الاسم. <button class="hm-faq-guide" data-guide-target="#hmColors" type="button">ورّيني الألوان</button></p></details>
<details><summary>واش التوصيل متوفر لجميع المدن وفشحال كيوصل؟ <i class="fas fa-plus"></i></summary><p>نعم، التوصيل متوفر لجميع المدن. غالباً كيوصل فـ48 ساعة، وقد يوصل حتى 3 أيام إلا كان ضغط على الطلبات.</p></details>
<details><summary>واش كاين تخفيض إلا طلبت أكثر من قطعة؟ <i class="fas fa-plus"></i></summary><p>نعم، من جوج قطع أو أكثر كيتطبق تخفيض 30 د.م. لأن التوصيل كيتجمع مرة واحدة، والمجموع كيتبدل مباشرة فالملخص. <button class="hm-faq-guide" data-guide-target="#hmQuantity" type="button">جرّبي الكمية</button></p></details>
<details><summary>كيفاش نعرف بلي طلبي تسجل؟ <i class="fas fa-plus"></i></summary><p>من بعد نجاح الإرسال غادي تبان صفحة شكر وملخص الطلب. وغادي توصلك رسالة فواتساب فيها الملخص وتحديثات حالة الطلب.</p></details>
<details><summary>واش غادي تتاصلوا بيا قبل الشحن؟ <i class="fas fa-plus"></i></summary><p>نعم، كنتاصلوا بك لتأكيد المنتج والمقاس واللون والمدينة والعنوان قبل الشحن.</p></details>
<details><summary>شنو ندير إلا ما تسجلش الطلب؟ <i class="fas fa-plus"></i></summary><p>إلا وقع عطل فالـWebhook، غادي يبان زر واتساب احتياطي فيه جميع معلومات الطلب جاهزة للإرسال.</p></details>
</div>

<script>

        const HM_PRODUCTS_URL = 'https://n8n.amalal.cloud/webhook/hijab-makka-store-products-v2';
        const HM_CATALOG_URLS = [HM_PRODUCTS_URL];
        const HM_ORDER_WEBHOOK_URL = 'https://n8n.amalal.cloud/webhook/makkah-store-order';
        const HM_COUPON_VALIDATE_URL = '';
        const HM_COUPON_REDEEM_URL = '';
        const HM_ORDER_STATUS_URL = 'https://n8n.amalal.cloud/webhook/hijab-makka-order-status';
        const HM_PRICE_REDUCTION_PERCENT = 0;

        function hmCreateEventId(prefix = 'hm') {
            if (window.crypto && typeof window.crypto.randomUUID === 'function') {
                return `${prefix}-${window.crypto.randomUUID()}`;
            }
            return `${prefix}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(16).slice(2)}`;
        }
        window.hmCreateEventId = hmCreateEventId;

        const HM_META_PIXEL_ID = '910477008108824';

        function hmTrackMeta(eventName, parameters = {}, eventId = '') {
            if (typeof window.fbq !== 'function') return false;
            const eventOptions = eventId ? { eventID: eventId } : {};
            if (HM_META_PIXEL_ID) {
                window.fbq('trackSingle', HM_META_PIXEL_ID, eventName, parameters, eventOptions);
            } else {
                window.fbq('track', eventName, parameters, eventOptions);
            }
            return true;
        }
        window.hmTrackMeta = hmTrackMeta;

        function hmReadCookie(name) {
            return (`; ${document.cookie || ''}`).split(`; ${name}=`).pop().split(';').shift() || '';
        }

        function hmGetVisitorTracking() {
            const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            const storageKey = 'hm_visitor_tracking_v2';
            let persistent = {};
            let session = {};
            try { persistent = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem(storageKey) || '{}'); } catch (e) {}
            try { session = JSON.parse(window.sessionStorage.getItem(storageKey) || '{}'); } catch (e) {}
            const now = Date.now();
            const sessionAge = now - new Date(session.last_activity || session.first_seen || 0).getTime();
            const sessionIsFresh = Boolean(session.session_id) && Number.isFinite(sessionAge) && sessionAge < 30 * 60 * 1000;
            const get = (...keys) => {
                for (const key of keys) {
                    const value = params.get(key) || session[key] || persistent[key] || '';
                    if (value) return value;
                }
                return '';
            };
            const ref = session.referrer || persistent.referrer || document.referrer || '';
            const fbclid = get('fbclid');
            const sourceHint = (fbclid || /facebook|instagram|fb\.com|l\.facebook/i.test(ref)) ? 'meta' : '';
            const derivedFbc = fbclid ? `fb.1.${Math.floor(now / 1000)}.${fbclid}` : '';
            const tracking = {
                session_id: sessionIsFresh ? session.session_id : hmCreateEventId('session'),
                visitor_id: persistent.visitor_id || session.visitor_id || hmCreateEventId('visitor'),
                first_seen: sessionIsFresh ? session.first_seen : new Date().toISOString(),
                landing_page: sessionIsFresh ? (session.landing_page || window.location.href) : window.location.href,
                current_page: window.location.href,
                referrer: ref,
                source: get('utm_source', 'source') || sourceHint || 'direct',
                medium: get('utm_medium', 'medium') || (sourceHint ? 'paid_social' : ''),
                campaign: get('utm_campaign', 'campaign_name', 'campaign') || '',
                content: get('utm_content', 'ad_name', 'ad') || '',
                term: get('utm_term') || '',
                utm_source: get('utm_source', 'source') || sourceHint || 'direct',
                utm_medium: get('utm_medium', 'medium') || (sourceHint ? 'paid_social' : ''),
                utm_campaign: get('utm_campaign', 'campaign_name', 'campaign') || '',
                utm_content: get('utm_content', 'ad_name', 'ad') || '',
                utm_term: get('utm_term') || '',
                utm_id: get('utm_id', 'campaign_id'),
                campaign_id: get('campaign_id', 'utm_id'),
                adset_id: get('adset_id'),
                ad_id: get('ad_id', 'utm_ad_id'),
                campaign_name: get('campaign_name', 'utm_campaign'),
                adset_name: get('adset_name'),
                ad_name: get('ad_name', 'utm_content'),
                placement: get('placement'),
                site_source_name: get('site_source_name'),
                fbclid,
                fbc: hmReadCookie('_fbc') || session.fbc || persistent.fbc || derivedFbc,
                fbp: hmReadCookie('_fbp') || session.fbp || persistent.fbp || '',
                product: params.get('product') || session.product || persistent.product || '',
                category: params.get('category') || session.category || persistent.category || '',
                tracked_at: new Date().toISOString(),
                last_activity: new Date().toISOString()
            };

            try {
                window.localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(tracking));
                window.sessionStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(tracking));
            } catch (e) {}
            return tracking;
        }

        function hmTrackOnce(key, eventName, parameters = {}, eventId = '') {
            const storageKey = `hm_event_${key}`;
            try {
                if (window.sessionStorage.getItem(storageKey)) return false;
            } catch (e) {}
            const resolvedEventId = eventId || hmCreateEventId(String(eventName || 'meta').toLowerCase());
            const sent = hmTrackMeta(eventName, parameters, resolvedEventId);
            if (sent) {
                try { window.sessionStorage.setItem(storageKey, resolvedEventId); } catch (e) {}
            }
            return sent;
        }
        window.hmTrackOnce = hmTrackOnce;

        function hmNotifyNewVisitor() {
            const allowedHosts = ['hijab-makka.store', 'www.hijab-makka.store'];
            const isLocalTest = ['file:', 'http:'].includes(window.location.protocol) && /^(localhost|127\.0\.0\.1)?$/.test(window.location.hostname || '');
            if (!allowedHosts.includes(window.location.hostname) && !isLocalTest) return;
            const tracking = hmGetVisitorTracking();
            const trafficSource = (isLocalTest && (!tracking.source || tracking.source === 'direct'))
                ? 'local_test'
                : (tracking.source || tracking.utm_source || 'Direct');
            const product = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('product') || '';
            fetch('https://n8n.amalal.cloud/webhook/hijab-makka-visitor', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                keepalive: true,
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    ...tracking,
                    source: 'hijab-makka-store',
                    traffic_source: trafficSource,
                    source_platform: trafficSource,
                    test_mode: isLocalTest,
                    session_id: tracking.session_id,
                    page_url: window.location.href,
                    product: product,
                    event_type: product ? 'product_view' : 'page_view',
                    event_time: new Date().toISOString(),
                    first_seen: tracking.first_seen || new Date().toISOString(),
                    page_title: document.title || '',
                    device: /tablet|ipad/i.test(navigator.userAgent || '')
                        ? 'tablet'
                        : (/mobile|android|iphone/i.test(navigator.userAgent || '') ? 'mobile' : 'desktop'),
                    user_agent: navigator.userAgent || ''
                })
            }).catch(function () {});
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', hmNotifyNewVisitor, { once: true });

        function hmSendVisitorEvent(eventType, data = {}) {
            const eventMap = {
                product_view: 'product_view',
                smart_product_landing: 'product_view',
                smart_quick_view: 'product_view',
                quick_view_open: 'product_view',
                add_to_order: 'add_to_order',
                order_started: 'order_started',
                order_submitted: 'order_submitted',
                order_whatsapp_fallback: 'whatsapp_click',
                newsletter_whatsapp_click: 'whatsapp_click',
                whatsapp_click: 'whatsapp_click',
                chat_open: 'chat_open',
                chat_message: 'chat_message',
                product_selected: 'product_selected',
                checkout_step: 'checkout_step',
                field_started: 'field_started'
            };
            const mapped = eventMap[eventType];
            if (!mapped) return false;
            const allowedHosts = ['hijab-makka.store', 'www.hijab-makka.store'];
            const isLocalTest = ['file:', 'http:'].includes(window.location.protocol) && /^(localhost|127\.0\.0\.1)?$/.test(window.location.hostname || '');
            if (!allowedHosts.includes(window.location.hostname) && !isLocalTest) return false;
            const tracking = hmGetVisitorTracking();
            const trafficSource = (isLocalTest && (!tracking.source || tracking.source === 'direct'))
                ? 'local_test'
                : (tracking.source || tracking.utm_source || 'Direct');
            const product = data.product_id || tracking.product || new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('product') || '';
            const dedupeKey = [mapped, product, data.journey_step || '', data.field_name || '', data.query || ''].join('|');
            const dedupeWindow = ({ product_view: 45000, product_selected: 30000, checkout_step: 120000, field_started: 600000, chat_open: 120000, chat_message: 2500 })[mapped] || 1500;
            try {
                const sentEvents = JSON.parse(window.sessionStorage.getItem('hm_visitor_event_dedupe_v1') || '{}');
                const lastSent = Number(sentEvents[dedupeKey] || 0);
                if (Date.now() - lastSent < dedupeWindow) return false;
                sentEvents[dedupeKey] = Date.now();
                Object.keys(sentEvents).forEach(key => {
                    if (Date.now() - Number(sentEvents[key] || 0) > 30 * 60 * 1000) delete sentEvents[key];
                });
                window.sessionStorage.setItem('hm_visitor_event_dedupe_v1', JSON.stringify(sentEvents));
            } catch (_) {}
            try {
                fetch('https://n8n.amalal.cloud/webhook/hijab-makka-visitor', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    keepalive: true,
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        ...tracking,
                        ...data,
                        source: 'hijab-makka-store',
                        traffic_source: data.traffic_source || trafficSource,
                        source_platform: data.traffic_source || trafficSource,
                        test_mode: isLocalTest,
                        event_type: mapped,
                        event_time: new Date().toISOString(),
                        page_url: window.location.href,
                        page_title: document.title || '',
                        product_id: product,
                        product: product,
                        product_name: data.product_name || window.HM_SELECTED_PRODUCT?.name || '',
                        device: /tablet|ipad/i.test(navigator.userAgent || '') ? 'tablet' : (/mobile|android|iphone/i.test(navigator.userAgent || '') ? 'mobile' : 'desktop'),
                        user_agent: navigator.userAgent || ''
                    })
                }).catch(function () {});
                return true;
            } catch (_) {
                return false;
            }
        }
        window.hmSendVisitorEvent = hmSendVisitorEvent;

        function hmJourneyProductData(extra = {}) {
            const product = window.HM_SELECTED_PRODUCT || {};
            const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            const productId = extra.product_id || product.product_id || product.id || params.get('product') || '';
            return {
                product_id: productId,
                product: productId,
                product_name: extra.product_name || product.name || '',
                supplier: extra.supplier || product.supplier || '',
                category_slug: extra.category_slug || product.category_slug || params.get('category') || '',
                journey_from: extra.journey_from || window.HM_LAST_JOURNEY_FROM || '',
                ...extra
            };
        }

        function hmTrackJourney(eventType, extra = {}) {
            try {
                if (typeof window.hmSendVisitorEvent === 'function') {
                    window.hmSendVisitorEvent(eventType, hmJourneyProductData(extra));
                }
            } catch (_) {}
        }
        window.hmTrackJourney = hmTrackJourney;

        function hmGetAttribution() {
            const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            const keys = ['fbclid', 'fbc', 'fbp', 'utm_source', 'utm_medium', 'utm_campaign', 'utm_content', 'utm_term', 'utm_id', 'campaign_id', 'campaign_name', 'adset_id', 'adset_name', 'ad_id', 'ad_name', 'placement', 'site_source_name'];
            const attribution = {};
            const tracking = hmGetVisitorTracking();

            keys.forEach((key) => {
                const incomingValue = params.get(key);
                if (incomingValue) sessionStorage.setItem(`hm_${key}`, incomingValue);
                attribution[key] = incomingValue || sessionStorage.getItem(`hm_${key}`) || tracking[key] || '';
            });
            attribution.fbc = attribution.fbc || tracking.fbc || hmReadCookie('_fbc') || '';
            attribution.fbp = attribution.fbp || tracking.fbp || hmReadCookie('_fbp') || '';

            return attribution;
        }

        function hmText(value) {
            return String(value ?? '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
        }

        function hmNumericId(value) {
            return String(value || '').match(/\d+/)?.[0] || '';
        }

        function hmNormalizeArabic(value) {
            return hmText(value)
                .normalize('NFKC')
                .replace(/[ًٌٍَُِّْـ]/g, '')
                .replace(/[أإآ]/g, 'ا')
                .replace(/ى/g, 'ي')
                .toLowerCase();
        }


        function hmCategorySlug(value) {
            return hmNormalizeArabic(value)
                .normalize('NFD')
                .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
                .replace(/[^a-z0-9\u0600-\u06ff]+/g, '-')
                .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '') || 'other';
        }

        function hmCategoryLabel(product) {
            return hmText(
                product?.category_ar ||
                product?.category_name_ar ||
                product?.category ||
                product?.category_name ||
                'منتجات أخرى'
            ) || 'منتجات أخرى';
        }

        function hmEscapeHtml(value) {
            return String(value ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, function(char) {
                return {
                    '&': '&amp;',
                    '<': '&lt;',
                    '>': '&gt;',
                    '"': '&quot;',
                    "'": '&#039;'
                }[char];
            });
        }

        function hmColorHex(name) {
            const key = hmNormalizeArabic(name);
            const exactMap = new Map([
                ['\u0627\u0633\u0648\u062f', '#0f172a'],
                ['\u0633\u0645\u0627\u0648\u064a', '#7dd3fc'],
                ['\u0643\u0627\u0634\u064a\u0631\u064a', '#d7a6aa'],
                ['\u0628\u0644\u0648\u0645\u0627\u0631\u064a\u0646', '#1f3b57'],
                ['\u0632\u064a\u062a\u064a', '#4f6f52'],
                ['\u0627\u062e\u0636\u0631 \u0632\u064a\u062a\u064a', '#4f6f52'],
                ['\u0627\u0632\u0631\u0642', '#2563eb'],
                ['\u0644\u064a\u0645\u0648\u0646\u064a', '#d9f99d'],
                ['\u0641\u0648\u0634\u064a\u0627', '#d946ef'],
                ['\u0635\u0627\u0628\u0644\u064a', '#d6b58c'],
                ['\u0627\u0632\u0631\u0642 \u0645\u062e\u0636\u0631', '#0f766e']
            ]);
            if (exactMap.has(key)) return exactMap.get(key);
            const map = [
                [/noir|black|اسود|كحل/, '#111111'],
                [/blanc|white|ابيض|بيضا/, '#ffffff'],
                [/bleu marine|navy|كحلي/, '#14213d'],
                [/bleu|blue|ازرق|زرقا/, '#2563eb'],
                [/rouge|red|احمر|حمرا|bordeaux|bordaux|بوردو/, '#8B0000'],
                [/rose|pink|وردي|روز/, '#ec4899'],
                [/vert|green|اخضر|خضرا|زيتي/, '#16a34a'],
                [/gris|gray|grey|رمادي|كري|ڭري/, '#6b7280'],
                [/beige|بيج/, '#d6b58c'],
                [/marron|brown|بني|قهوي|قهوة/, '#7c3f1d'],
                [/violet|purple|mauve|بنفسجي|موف|move/, '#7c3aed'],
                [/orange|برتقالي/, '#f97316'],
                [/jaune|yellow|اصفر/, '#eab308']
            ];
            for (const [re, hex] of map) if (re.test(key)) return hex;
            return '#cccccc';
        }

        function hmImageTokensForColor(name) {
            const key = hmNormalizeArabic(name);
            if (/سماوي|bleuciel|sky/.test(key)) return ['bleuciel', 'sky', 'cyan'];
            if (/بلومارين|كحلي|navy|marine/.test(key)) return ['navy', 'marine', 'bleu-marine', 'bleumarine'];
            if (/كاشيري|وردي|روز|pink|rose/.test(key)) return ['pink', 'rose', 'cashmere', 'kashiri'];
            if (/زيتي|olive|kaki|khaki/.test(key)) return ['olive', 'kaki', 'khaki', 'zayti'];
            if (/اسود|كحل|black|noir/.test(key)) return ['black', 'noir'];
            if (/ازرق|blue|bleu/.test(key)) return ['blue-', 'blue_', 'blue.'];
            if (/ليموني|lemon|lime/.test(key)) return ['lemon', 'lime', 'orange', 'yellow', 'jaune'];
            if (/فوشيا|fuchsia|fushia/.test(key)) return ['fuchsia', 'fushia', 'rosefushia', 'pink'];
            if (/اصفر|yellow|jaune/.test(key)) return ['yellow', 'jaune'];
            if (/برتقالي|orange/.test(key)) return ['orange'];
            return [];
        }

        function hmImageForColor(color, images = []) {
            const explicit = color && (color.image || color.image_url || color.imageUrl);
            if (explicit) return explicit;
            const name = color?.label || color?.name || color?.color || '';
            const tokens = hmImageTokensForColor(name);
            if (!tokens.length || !Array.isArray(images)) return '';
            const colorImages = images.filter(src => {
                const file = String(src || '').split('/').pop().toLowerCase();
                return file && !file.includes('default') && tokens.some(token => file.includes(token));
            });
            return colorImages[0] || '';
        }

        function hmNormalizeLiveProduct(product) {
            return typeof window.hmNormalizeLiveProductImpl === 'function'
                ? window.hmNormalizeLiveProductImpl(product)
                : product;
        }

        // ============================================
        // 1. جلب كل المنتجات من الرابط
        // ============================================
        async function loadAllProducts() {
            const container = document.getElementById('hm-borkini-app');
            container.innerHTML = '';
            window.HM_PRODUCTS_READY = false;
            window.HM_PRODUCTS_LOAD_ERROR = false;
            window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('hm:products-loading'));

            try {
                const catalogResults = await Promise.allSettled(HM_CATALOG_URLS.map(async (url) => {
                    const controller = new AbortController();
                    const requestTimeout = window.setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 12000);
                    try {
                        const response = await fetch(url, { cache: 'no-store', signal: controller.signal });
                        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`${url} رجع HTTP ${response.status}`);
                        return response.json();
                    } catch (requestError) {
                        if (requestError?.name === 'AbortError') throw new Error(`${url} تجاوز مهلة التحميل`);
                        throw requestError;
                    } finally {
                        window.clearTimeout(requestTimeout);
                    }
                }));
                const payloads = catalogResults
                    .filter(result => result.status === 'fulfilled')
                    .map(result => result.value);
                if (!payloads.length) {
                    const reasons = catalogResults.map(result => result.reason?.message).filter(Boolean).join(' | ');
                    throw new Error(reasons || 'تعذر تحميل مصادر الكتالوج');
                }
                const data = payloads[0] || {};
                const rawProducts = payloads.flatMap(payload => Array.isArray(payload)
                    ? payload
                    : (Array.isArray(payload?.products) ? payload.products : []));
                const categoryMap = new Map();
                payloads.flatMap(payload => Array.isArray(payload?.categories) ? payload.categories : []).forEach(category => {
                    const slug = String(category?.slug || category?.category_slug || '').trim();
                    if (slug && !categoryMap.has(slug)) categoryMap.set(slug, category);
                });
                window.HM_API_CATEGORIES = [...categoryMap.values()];

                if (rawProducts.length) {
                    const productId = getProductIdFromURL();
                    const liveProducts = rawProducts
                        .map(hmNormalizeLiveProduct)
                        .filter(product => product && product.name && Number(product.price) > 0);

                    const seenProductIds = new Set();
                    window.HM_PRODUCTS = liveProducts.filter(product => {
                        const supplierKey=String(product.supplier||'unknown').trim().toLowerCase();
                        const supplierProductKey=String(
                            product.supplier_product_id||
                            product.original_id||
                            product.product_id||
                            product.id||
                            ''
                        ).trim().toLowerCase();
                        const key=`${supplierKey}:${supplierProductKey}`;
                        if (!supplierProductKey || seenProductIds.has(key)) return false;
                        seenProductIds.add(key);
                        return true;
                    }).sort((a, b) => {
                        const categoryCompare = String(a?.category_ar || a?.category || '')
                            .localeCompare(String(b?.category_ar || b?.category || ''), 'ar');
                        if (categoryCompare !== 0) return categoryCompare;
                        const supplierPriority = { chic: 0, chic_affiliate: 0, affilo: 1, manual: 2 };
                        const aPriority = supplierPriority[String(a?.supplier || '').toLowerCase()] ?? 9;
                        const bPriority = supplierPriority[String(b?.supplier || '').toLowerCase()] ?? 9;
                        return aPriority - bPriority;
                    });

                    renderAllProducts(window.HM_PRODUCTS, { showImmediately: true });

                    if (productId) {
                        // الاختيار التلقائي يتم فقط عندما يحمل رابط الإعلان معرّف منتج واضحاً.
                        setTimeout(() => { window.selectProduct(productId); }, 250);
                    }

                } else {
                    const localProducts = Array.isArray(HM_LOCAL_PRODUCTS)
                        ? HM_LOCAL_PRODUCTS.map(hmNormalizeLiveProduct).filter(product => product && product.name && Number(product.price) > 0)
                        : [];
                    if (localProducts.length) {
                        const productId = getProductIdFromURL();
                        window.HM_PRODUCTS = localProducts.sort((a, b) => {
                            const supplierPriority = { chic: 0, affilo: 1, manual: 2 };
                            return (supplierPriority[String(a?.supplier || '').toLowerCase()] ?? 9) - (supplierPriority[String(b?.supplier || '').toLowerCase()] ?? 9);
                        });
                        renderAllProducts(window.HM_PRODUCTS, { showImmediately: true });
                        if (productId) {
                            setTimeout(() => { window.selectProduct(productId); }, 250);
                        }
                    } else {
                        container.innerHTML = `<div style="padding:35px;text-align:center;color:#b00020;font-weight:900;"><i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> ${data?.message || 'لم يتم العثور على منتجات متاحة حالياً'}</div>`;
                    }
                }
            } catch (error) {
                window.HM_PRODUCTS_LOAD_ERROR = true;
                const localProducts = Array.isArray(HM_LOCAL_PRODUCTS)
                    ? HM_LOCAL_PRODUCTS.map(hmNormalizeLiveProduct).filter(product => product && product.name && Number(product.price) > 0)
                    : [];
                if (localProducts.length) {
                    const productId = getProductIdFromURL();
                    window.HM_PRODUCTS = localProducts.sort((a, b) => {
                        const supplierPriority = { chic: 0, affilo: 1, manual: 2 };
                        return (supplierPriority[String(a?.supplier || '').toLowerCase()] ?? 9) - (supplierPriority[String(b?.supplier || '').toLowerCase()] ?? 9);
                    });
                    renderAllProducts(window.HM_PRODUCTS, { showImmediately: true });
                    if (productId) {
                        setTimeout(() => { window.selectProduct(productId); }, 250);
                    }
                } else {
                    container.innerHTML = `<div style="padding:35px;text-align:center;color:#b00020;font-weight:900;"><i class="fas fa-exclamation-circle"></i> فشل تحميل المنتجات. حاولي تحديث الصفحة.</div>`;
                    console.error('خطأ:', error);
                }
            } finally {
                window.HM_PRODUCTS_READY = true;
                const loadedCount = Array.isArray(window.HM_PRODUCTS) ? window.HM_PRODUCTS.length : 0;
                window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('hm:products-ready', {
                    detail: {
                        count: loadedCount,
                        hasProducts: loadedCount > 0,
                        usedFallback: Boolean(window.HM_PRODUCTS_LOAD_ERROR && loadedCount > 0),
                        failed: Boolean(window.HM_PRODUCTS_LOAD_ERROR && loadedCount === 0)
                    }
                }));
            }
        }

        // ============================================
        // 2. قراءة المنتج من رابط الصفحة
        // ============================================
        function getProductIdFromURL() {
            const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);

            // الرابط القياسي: ?product=chic202
            const standardProduct = hmText(params.get('product'));
            if (standardProduct) return standardProduct;

            // روابط الإعلانات حسب التصنيف، مثال:
            // ?BOURKINIproduct=chic202
            for (const [key, value] of params.entries()) {
                if (/product$/i.test(hmText(key)) && hmText(value)) {
                    return hmText(value);
                }
            }

            // بدون معرّف منتج: نعرض التصنيف ونترك الاختيار للعميل.
            return '';
        }
        window.hmGetProductIdFromURL = getProductIdFromURL;

        // ============================================
        // 3. عرض كل المنتجات مع أيقونات Font Awesome
        // ============================================
        function renderAllProducts(products, options = {}) {
            const appContainer = document.getElementById('hm-borkini-app');
            const toggleWrapper = document.getElementById('hmModelsToggleWrapper');
            const productsList = document.getElementById('hmProductsList');
            const productCount = products.length;
            const showImmediately = Boolean(options.showImmediately);

            // لا نعرض المنتجات فوق قسم الطلب. هذا المكان يبقى مخفياً فقط للتحميل.
            if (appContainer) appContainer.innerHTML = '';

            if (!productsList || !toggleWrapper) return;

            productsList.style.setProperty('display', showImmediately ? 'block' : 'none', 'important');
            productsList.className = '';

            const categoryMap = new Map();
            products.forEach(product => {
                const slug = hmText(product.category_slug) || hmCategorySlug(product.category_ar || product.category);
                const label = hmText(product.category_ar || product.category) || 'منتجات أخرى';
                if (!categoryMap.has(slug)) categoryMap.set(slug, { slug, label, count: 0 });
                categoryMap.get(slug).count += 1;
            });

            const categories = Array.from(categoryMap.values()).sort((a, b) =>
                b.count - a.count || a.label.localeCompare(b.label, 'ar')
            );

            const filterBar = document.querySelector('.hm-filter-bar');
            if (filterBar) {
                filterBar.innerHTML = [
                    `<button class="hm-filter active" data-filter="all" type="button">الكل (${productCount})</button>`,
                    ...categories.map(category =>
                        `<button class="hm-filter" data-filter="${hmEscapeHtml(category.slug)}" type="button">${hmEscapeHtml(category.label)} (${category.count})</button>`
                    )
                ].join('');
            }

            let html = `
                <div id="hmProductsCatalogTitle">
                    <h2>جميع منتجات HIJAB MAKKAH</h2>
                    <p>اختاري التصنيف ثم المنتج، وغادي يتبدل قسم الطلب تلقائياً بالصورة والمقاسات والألوان والثمن.</p>
                    <div style="margin-top:9px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;background:#eaf8ef;color:#20844f;border-radius:999px;padding:7px 12px;font-size:12px;font-weight:900;">
                        <i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> ${productCount} منتج متاح للطلب
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="products-grid" id="hmLiveProductsGrid">
            `;

            products.forEach((product) => {
                const colors = Array.isArray(product.colors) ? product.colors : [];
                const sizes = Array.isArray(product.sizes) ? product.sizes : [];
                const visibleColors = colors.slice(0, 4);
                const dots = visibleColors.map(color => {
                    const hex = color.hex || hmColorHex(color.label || color.name || '');
                    const title = color.label || color.name || '';
                    return `<span class="hm-color-chip" title="${title.replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}"><span class="hm-color-dot" style="background:${hex};"></span><b>${title}</b></span>`;
                }).join('');
                const moreColors = colors.length > 4 ? `<span class="hm-more-colors">+${colors.length - 4}</span>` : '';
                const supplierLabel = String(product.supplier || '').toLowerCase().includes('affilo') ? 'Affilo' : (String(product.supplier || '').toLowerCase().includes('chic') ? 'Chic' : 'HIJAB MAKKAH');
                const oldPrice = Number(product.compare_at_price || product.original_price || 0);
                const currentPrice = Number(product.price || 0);
                const priceHtml = oldPrice && oldPrice > currentPrice
                    ? `<span style="text-decoration:line-through;color:#999;font-size:12px;">${oldPrice} د.م.</span><br><span>${currentPrice} د.م.</span>`
                    : `${currentPrice} د.م.`;
                const img = product.main_image || product.primary_image_url || (product.images && product.images[0]) || '';
                const categoryLabel = product.category_ar || product.category || 'منتجات أخرى';
                const categorySlug = product.category_slug || hmCategorySlug(categoryLabel);

                html += `
                    <div class="product-card" data-id="${hmEscapeHtml(product.id)}" data-category="${hmEscapeHtml(categorySlug)}" data-pieces="${/4\s*قطع|4\s*pcs/i.test(product.name + ' ' + (product.description || '')) ? '4' : (/3\s*قطع|3\s*pcs/i.test(product.name + ' ' + (product.description || '')) ? '3' : '')}" data-plus="${sizes.some(s => /2XL|3XL|4XL/i.test(String(s)))}" onclick="selectProduct('${product.id}')">
                        <img referrerpolicy="no-referrer" src="${img}" alt="${product.name}"
                             loading="lazy"
                             onerror="this.onerror=null;this.src='data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg xmlns=%22http://www.w3.org/2000/svg%22 width=%22300%22 height=%22380%22%3E%3Crect width=%22300%22 height=%22380%22 fill=%22%23f0e8e4%22/%3E%3Ctext x=%22150%22 y=%22190%22 text-anchor=%22middle%22 fill=%22%23999%22 font-size=%2220%22%3EHIJAB MAKKA%3C/text%3E%3C/svg%3E'">
                        <div class="card-body">
                            <div class="hm-card-topline"><span><i class="fas fa-circle-check"></i> متوفر الآن</span><em>${hmEscapeHtml(categoryLabel)}</em></div>
                            <h3>${hmEscapeHtml(product.name)}</h3>
                            <div class="price">${priceHtml}</div>
                            <div class="hm-product-colors">${dots}${moreColors}</div>
                            ${sizes.length ? `<div style="font-size:11px;color:#5f514d;margin-top:7px;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;">
                                <i class="fas fa-ruler"></i> ${sizes.slice(0, 6).join(' - ')}
                            </div>` : ''}
                            <button type="button" style="margin-top:9px;width:100%;border:0;background:#7f1734;color:#fff;border-radius:10px;padding:9px;font-size:12px;font-weight:900;cursor:pointer;">
                                اختاري هذا المنتج
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
            });

            html += '</div>';
            productsList.innerHTML = html;

            // تبديل صور المنتج عند المرور بالماوس، وعند اللمس في الهاتف
            productsList.querySelectorAll('.product-card').forEach((card) => {
                const product = products.find(p => String(p.id) === String(card.dataset.id));
                const imageEl = card.querySelector('img');
                if (!product || !imageEl) return;

                const images = [...new Set([
                    product.main_image,
                    product.primary_image_url,
                    ...(Array.isArray(product.images) ? product.images : []),
                    ...(Array.isArray(product.colors) ? product.colors.map(c => c.image) : [])
                ].filter(Boolean))];

                if (images.length < 2) return;
                let imageIndex = Math.max(0, images.indexOf(imageEl.getAttribute('src')));
                let hoverTimer = null;

                const showImage = (nextIndex) => {
                    imageIndex = (nextIndex + images.length) % images.length;
                    imageEl.style.opacity = '0.25';
                    window.setTimeout(() => {
                        imageEl.src = images[imageIndex];
                        imageEl.style.opacity = '1';
                    }, 120);
                };

                card.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
                    if (window.matchMedia('(hover: hover)').matches) {
                        showImage(imageIndex + 1);
                        hoverTimer = window.setInterval(() => showImage(imageIndex + 1), 950);
                    }
                });

                card.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
                    if (hoverTimer) window.clearInterval(hoverTimer);
                    hoverTimer = null;
                    showImage(0);
                });

                card.addEventListener('touchstart', () => {
                    showImage(imageIndex + 1);
                }, { passive: true });
            });

            if (showImmediately) {
                toggleWrapper.style.display = 'none';
                toggleWrapper.innerHTML = '';
            } else {
                toggleWrapper.style.display = 'block';
                toggleWrapper.innerHTML = `
                    <button id="hmToggleModelsBtn" type="button" onclick="hmToggleOtherModels()" style="border:0;background:#7f1734;color:#fff;border-radius:999px;padding:12px 18px;font-size:14px;font-weight:900;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:0 10px 24px rgba(127,23,52,.16);">
                        <i class="fas fa-layer-group"></i> إظهار المنتجات الأخرى (${productCount})
                    </button>
                `;
            }
        }

        function hmToggleOtherModels() {
            const productsList = document.getElementById('hmProductsList');
            const btn = document.getElementById('hmToggleModelsBtn');
            if (!productsList || !btn) return;

            const isHidden = productsList.style.display === 'none' || getComputedStyle(productsList).display === 'none';
            if (isHidden) {
                productsList.style.setProperty('display', 'block', 'important');
                btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-eye-slash"></i> إخفاء المنتجات الأخرى';
                productsList.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
            } else {
                productsList.style.setProperty('display', 'none', 'important');
                btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-layer-group"></i> إظهار المنتجات الأخرى (' + (window.HM_PRODUCTS?.length || 0) + ')';
            }
        }

        // ============================================
        // 4. دالة تحديد منتج (معدلة لدعم hex واسم اللون)
        // ============================================
        function hmSetPressed(containerSelector, activeButton) {
            document.querySelectorAll(containerSelector + ' .choice-btn').forEach(btn => {
                btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
                btn.style.borderColor = '#ddd';
                btn.style.background = '#fff';
                btn.style.color = '';
            });
            if (activeButton) {
                activeButton.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
                activeButton.style.borderColor = '#7f1734';
                activeButton.style.background = '#fff3f6';
                activeButton.style.color = '#7f1734';
            }
        }

        function hmFormatPrice(product) {
            const current = Number(product?.price || 0);
            const old = Number(product?.compare_at_price || product?.original_price || 0);
            if (old && old > current) {
                return `<span style="display:inline-block;text-decoration:line-through;color:#a99a95;font-size:18px;margin-inline-start:8px;">${old} د.م.</span><span>${current} د.م.</span>`;
            }
            return `<span>${current} د.م.</span>`;
        }

        function hmUpdateOrderAssistant() {
            const product = window.HM_SELECTED_PRODUCT || null;
            const size = document.getElementById('hmSelectedSize')?.value || '';
            const color = document.getElementById('hmSelectedColor')?.value || '';
            const needsSize=Boolean(product?.has_size_options);
            const needsColor=Boolean(product?.has_color_options);
            const sizeReady=!needsSize||Boolean(size);
            const colorReady=!needsColor||Boolean(color);
            const name = document.getElementById('hmName')?.value.trim() || '';
            const phone = document.getElementById('hmPhone')?.value.trim() || '';
            const citySelect = document.getElementById('hmCity');
            const city = citySelect?.selectedOptions?.[0]?.textContent?.trim() || '';
            const address = document.getElementById('hmAddress')?.value.trim() || '';
            const quantity = Number(document.getElementById('hmQuantity')?.value || 1);
            const assistant = document.getElementById('hmAssistantText');
            const summary = document.getElementById('hmLiveSummary');
            const progress = document.getElementById('hmOrderProgress');
            const submitBtn = document.getElementById('hmSubmit');
            const phoneDigits = phone.replace(/\D/g, '');
            const nameParts = name.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
            const nameOk = name.length >= 2;
            const nameComplete = nameParts.length >= 2;
            const phoneOk = (/^0[67]\d{8}$/.test(phoneDigits) || /^212[67]\d{8}$/.test(phoneDigits)) && !/^(?:0[67]|212[67])(\d)\1{7}$/.test(phoneDigits);
            const cityOk = Boolean(citySelect?.value);
            const addressOk = address.length >= 10;
            const steps = {
                product: Boolean(product),
                choices: Boolean(product && sizeReady && colorReady),
                info: Boolean(nameOk && phoneOk && cityOk && addressOk),
                send: false
            };
            const choiceChecks=[product,needsSize?size:true,needsColor?color:true];
            const doneCount=[...choiceChecks,nameOk,phoneOk,cityOk,addressOk].filter(Boolean).length;
            const percent=Math.round((doneCount/(choiceChecks.length+4))*100);

            if (product && sizeReady && colorReady) {
                hmTrackOnce(
                    `add_to_cart_${product.id}_${size}_${color}`,
                    'AddToCart',
                    {
                        content_ids: [String(product.product_id || product.id)],
                        content_name: product.name || '',
                        content_type: 'product',
                        num_items: quantity,
                        value: Number(product.price || 0) * quantity,
                        currency: 'MAD'
                    }
                );
            }

            document.querySelectorAll('.hm-step').forEach(step => {
                const key = step.dataset.step;
                step.classList.remove('done', 'active');
                if (steps[key]) step.classList.add('done');
            });

            let active = 'product';
            let text = 'اختاري المنتج اللي عجبك من القائمة.';
            let buttonText = '<i class="fas fa-shopping-cart"></i> تأكيد الطلب';
            if (product && needsSize && !size) {
                active = 'choices';
                text = 'مزيان، دابا اختاري المقاس المناسب باش نكملو الطلب.';
                buttonText = '<i class="fas fa-ruler"></i> اختاري المقاس أولاً';
            } else if (product && sizeReady && needsColor && !color) {
                active = 'choices';
                text = 'باقي غير اللون. ملي تختاريه الصورة والملخص كيتبدلو مباشرة.';
                buttonText = '<i class="fas fa-palette"></i> اختاري اللون أولاً';
            } else if (product && sizeReady && colorReady && !(nameOk && phoneOk && cityOk && addressOk)) {
                active = 'info';
                const missing = [];
                if (!nameOk) missing.push('الاسم');
                if (!phoneOk) missing.push('الهاتف');
                if (!cityOk) missing.push('المدينة');
                if (!addressOk) missing.push('العنوان');
                text = `اختياراتك واجدة. باقي غير ${missing.join('، ')} باش نرسلو الطلب بشكل صحيح.`;
                buttonText = '<i class="fas fa-edit"></i> كمّلي المعلومات';
            } else if (product && sizeReady && colorReady && nameOk && phoneOk && cityOk && addressOk) {
                active = 'send';
                text = 'كلشي واجد. راجعي الملخص واضغطي على تأكيد الطلب، وغادي نتواصلو معك قبل الشحن.';
                buttonText = '<i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> تأكيد الطلب الآن';
            }

            document.querySelector(`.hm-step[data-step="${active}"]`)?.classList.add('active');
            if (assistant) assistant.textContent = text;
            if (progress) progress.style.width = `${percent}%`;
            if (submitBtn && !submitBtn.disabled) submitBtn.innerHTML = buttonText;

            const setHint = (id, ok, emptyText, goodText, warnText) => {
                const el = document.getElementById(id);
                if (!el) return;
                el.classList.toggle('good', ok);
                el.textContent = ok ? goodText : (warnText || emptyText);
            };
            setHint('hmNameHint', nameOk, 'الاسم ضروري؛ اسم واحد واضح مقبول، والاسم والنسب أفضل.', nameComplete ? 'الاسم والنسب واضحين، ممتاز.' : 'الاسم واضح ومقبول. زيدي النسب إذا أمكن باش ما يوقعش خلط.', name ? 'الاسم قصير بزاف.' : 'دخلي الاسم باش نقدروا نسجلو الطلب ونأكدوه.');
            setHint('hmPhoneHint', phoneOk, 'الهاتف ضروري ويُفضّل يكون خدام فالمكالمات وواتساب.', 'رقم صحيح. غادي نستعملوه للمكالمة ورسالة واتساب ديال الملخص والمستجدات.', phone ? 'دخلي 06/07 من 10 أرقام أو الصيغة الدولية 2126/2127.' : 'الهاتف ضروري ويُفضّل يكون خدام فالمكالمات وواتساب.');
            setHint('hmCityHint', cityOk, 'التوصيل مجاني، اختاري غير المدينة باش نعرفو فين نوصلو الطلب.', city ? `تم اختيار ${city} — التوصيل مجاني.` : 'تم اختيار المدينة — التوصيل مجاني.', 'اختاري المدينة باش نعرفو فين نوصلو الطلب.');
            setHint('hmAddressHint', addressOk, 'العنوان ضروري باش يوصلك الموصّل حتى باب الدار.', 'العنوان واضح، الموصّل يقدر يوصل حتى الباب.', address ? 'زيدي الحي، الشارع ورقم المنزل أو أقرب نقطة معروفة.' : 'العنوان ضروري باش يوصلك الموصّل حتى باب الدار.');

            if (summary && product) {
                const subtotal = Number(product.price || 0) * quantity;
                const previewItem = typeof orderItem === 'function' ? orderItem(product, size, color, quantity) : null;
                const previewItems = [...(Array.isArray(window.HM_ORDER_ITEMS)?window.HM_ORDER_ITEMS:[])];
                if(previewItem){const previewIndex=previewItems.findIndex(item=>item.key===previewItem.key);previewIndex>=0?previewItems[previewIndex]=previewItem:previewItems.push(previewItem);}
                const multiBuyDiscount = (typeof hmCartDiscountInfo === 'function' ? hmCartDiscountInfo(previewItems).amount : 0);
                const total = Math.max(0, subtotal - multiBuyDiscount);
                summary.style.display = 'block';
                summary.innerHTML = typeof window.hmRenderInvoiceSummary === 'function'
                    ? window.hmRenderInvoiceSummary({ product, size, color, quantity, subtotal, discount:multiBuyDiscount, total })
                    : `<strong>ملخص الطلب:</strong> ${product.name || ''} — ${total} د.م.`;
            } else if (summary) {
                summary.style.display = 'none';
            }
        }

        function selectProduct(productId, options = {}) {
            const product = window.HM_PRODUCTS?.find(p => p.id === productId || String(p.supplier_product_id) === String(productId).replace(/\D/g, ''));
            if (!product) return;
            window.HM_SELECTED_PRODUCT = product;
            window.HM_SELECTED_COLOR = null;

            hmTrackMeta('ViewContent', {
                content_ids: [String(product.product_id || product.id)],
                content_name: product.name || '',
                content_type: 'product',
                value: Number(product.price || 0),
                currency: 'MAD'
            });

            try {
                const currentUrl = new URL(window.location.href);
                currentUrl.searchParams.set('product', product.id);
                history.replaceState(null, '', currentUrl.toString());
            } catch (e) {}

            document.querySelectorAll('.product-card').forEach(card => {
                card.dataset.active = String(card.dataset.id === product.id);
            });

            document.getElementById('hmProductName').textContent = product.name;
            document.getElementById('hmProductCode').textContent = `${product.category_ar || product.category || 'منتج'} | ${String(product.supplier || '').toLowerCase().includes('affilo') ? 'Affilo' : (String(product.supplier || '').toLowerCase().includes('chic') ? 'Chic' : 'HIJAB MAKKAH')} | كود: ${product.id}`;
            document.getElementById('hmProductDescription').textContent = product.description || 'بوركيني محتشم وأنيق متوفر حسب المخزون.';
            document.getElementById('hmPrice').innerHTML = hmFormatPrice(product);
            const selectedMainImage = product.main_image || product.primary_image_url || (product.images && product.images[0]) || '';
            const mainImageEl = document.getElementById('hmMainImage');
            if (mainImageEl && selectedMainImage) {
                mainImageEl.referrerPolicy = 'no-referrer';
                mainImageEl.src = selectedMainImage;
                mainImageEl.alt = product.name || 'صورة البوركيني المختار';
                mainImageEl.style.display = 'block';
            }
            document.getElementById('hmInfoSummary').textContent = 'اختاري المنتج اللي عجبك بثقة: الصورة كتبدل حسب اللون، المقاسات المتوفرة واضحة، والثمن النهائي كيبان قبل تأكيد الطلب.';
            document.getElementById('hmMobileName').textContent = product.name;
            document.getElementById('hmMobilePrice').textContent = `${product.price} د.م.`;

            document.getElementById('hmSelectedSize').value = '';
            document.getElementById('hmSelectedColor').value = '';
            document.getElementById('hmSelectedColorId').value = '';

            const thumbsContainer = document.getElementById('hmThumbs');
            thumbsContainer.innerHTML = '';
            const productImages = Array.isArray(product.images) && product.images.length
                ? product.images
                : [product.main_image || product.primary_image_url].filter(Boolean);

            productImages.slice(0, 10).forEach((img, index) => {
                const thumb = document.createElement('img');
                thumb.src = img;
                thumb.referrerPolicy = 'no-referrer';
                thumb.className = 'thumb';
                thumb.dataset.active = index === 0 ? 'true' : 'false';
                thumb.style.width = '100%';
                thumb.style.height = '80px';
                thumb.style.objectFit = 'cover';
                thumb.style.borderRadius = '8px';
                thumb.style.cursor = 'pointer';
                thumb.style.border = '2px solid transparent';
                thumb.onclick = (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    document.getElementById('hmMainImage').src = img;
                    document.querySelectorAll('#hmThumbs img').forEach(t => t.dataset.active = 'false');
                    thumb.dataset.active = 'true';
                };
                thumbsContainer.appendChild(thumb);
            });

            const sizesContainer = document.getElementById('hmSizes');
            sizesContainer.innerHTML = '';
            (product.sizes || []).forEach(size => {
                const btn = document.createElement('button');
                btn.type = 'button';
                btn.textContent = size;
                btn.className = 'choice-btn';
                btn.onclick = () => {
                    hmSetPressed('#hmSizes', btn);
                    document.getElementById('hmSelectedSize').value = size;
                    hmUpdateOrderAssistant();
                };
                sizesContainer.appendChild(btn);
            });

            const colorsContainer = document.getElementById('hmColors');
            colorsContainer.innerHTML = '';
            (product.colors || []).forEach(color => {
                const btn = document.createElement('button');
                btn.type = 'button';
                btn.className = 'choice-btn';
                btn.style.display = 'flex';
                btn.style.alignItems = 'center';
                btn.style.gap = '8px';
                btn.style.width = '100%';
                btn.style.justifyContent = 'flex-start';

                const colorCircle = document.createElement('span');
                colorCircle.style.display = 'inline-block';
                colorCircle.style.width = '26px';
                colorCircle.style.height = '26px';
                colorCircle.style.borderRadius = '50%';
                colorCircle.style.border = '2px solid #ddd';
                colorCircle.style.flexShrink = '0';
                colorCircle.style.background = color.hex || hmColorHex(color.label || color.name || '');

                const colorName = document.createElement('span');
                colorName.textContent = color.label || color.name || 'التفاصيل عند التأكيد';

                btn.appendChild(colorCircle);
                btn.appendChild(colorName);

                btn.onclick = () => {
                    hmSetPressed('#hmColors', btn);
                    document.getElementById('hmSelectedColor').value = color.label || color.name || '';
                    document.getElementById('hmSelectedColorId').value = color.color_id || color.id || '';
                    const colorImage = hmImageForColor(color, product.images || []);
                    window.HM_SELECTED_COLOR = { ...color, image: colorImage || color.image || '' };
                    if (colorImage) {
                        document.getElementById('hmMainImage').src = colorImage;
                        document.querySelectorAll('#hmThumbs img').forEach(thumb => {
                            thumb.dataset.active = String(thumb.src === colorImage || thumb.getAttribute('src') === colorImage);
                        });
                    }
                    hmUpdateOrderAssistant();
                };
                colorsContainer.appendChild(btn);
            });

            hmUpdateOrderAssistant();

            if (!options.noScroll) {
                document.getElementById('hmOrder').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
            }
        }

        // ============================================
        // 5. تحميل المدن
        // ============================================

        async function loadCities() {
            const citiesSelect = document.getElementById('hmCity');
            if (!citiesSelect) return;

            const priorityNames = ['الدار البيضاء', 'الرباط', 'فاس', 'مراكش', 'أكادير', 'طنجة', 'مكناس', 'وجدة', 'سلا', 'تطوان', 'الجديدة', 'آسفي', 'القنيطرة'];
            const entries = Object.entries(HM_CITIES).map(([id, city]) => ({
                id,
                label: city.ar || city.original || id,
                original: city.original || ''
            }));

            entries.sort((a, b) => {
                const ai = priorityNames.indexOf(a.label);
                const bi = priorityNames.indexOf(b.label);
                if (ai !== -1 || bi !== -1) return (ai === -1 ? 999 : ai) - (bi === -1 ? 999 : bi);
                return a.label.localeCompare(b.label, 'ar');
            });

            citiesSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">اختاري المدينة</option>';
            entries.forEach(city => {
                const option = document.createElement('option');
                option.value = city.id;
                option.textContent = city.label;
                option.dataset.original = city.original;
                citiesSelect.appendChild(option);
            });
        }

        // ============================================
        // 6. معالجة نموذج الطلب عبر Webhook (تم الإصلاح)
        // ============================================
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
            const form = document.getElementById('hmOrderForm');
            const messageDiv = document.getElementById('hmMessage');
            const WEBHOOK_URL = HM_ORDER_WEBHOOK_URL;
            const visitorTracking = hmGetVisitorTracking();

            form.addEventListener('focusin', function() {
                const product = window.HM_SELECTED_PRODUCT || {};
                hmTrackOnce(`initiate_checkout_${product.product_id || product.id || 'unknown'}`, 'InitiateCheckout', {
                    content_ids: product.id ? [String(product.product_id || product.id)] : [],
                    content_name: product.name || '',
                    content_type: 'product',
                    num_items: Number(document.getElementById('hmQuantity')?.value || 1),
                    value: Number(product.price || 0),
                    currency: 'MAD'
                });
                if (!window.HM_CHECKOUT_FOCUS_TRACKED) {
                    window.HM_CHECKOUT_FOCUS_TRACKED = true;
                    try { window.hmTrackJourney?.('checkout_step', { journey_step: 'checkout_focus', product_id: product.id || '', product_name: product.name || '', supplier: product.supplier || '' }); } catch (_) {}
                }
            });

            ['hmName','hmPhone','hmCity','hmAddress','hmQuantity','hmNotes'].forEach(id => {
                const el = document.getElementById(id);
                if (!el) return;
                const fieldName = id.replace(/^hm/, '').toLowerCase();
                const trackFieldStart = () => {
                    window.HM_STARTED_FIELDS = window.HM_STARTED_FIELDS || {};
                    if (window.HM_STARTED_FIELDS[id]) return;
                    window.HM_STARTED_FIELDS[id] = true;
                    const product = window.HM_SELECTED_PRODUCT || {};
                    try { window.hmTrackJourney?.('field_started', { journey_step: 'field_started', field_name: fieldName, product_id: product.id || '', product_name: product.name || '', supplier: product.supplier || '' }); } catch (_) {}
                };
                el.addEventListener('input', function () { trackFieldStart(); hmUpdateOrderAssistant(); });
                el.addEventListener('change', function () { trackFieldStart(); hmUpdateOrderAssistant(); });
            });

            form.addEventListener('submit', async function(e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                
                const name = document.getElementById('hmName').value.trim();
                const phone = document.getElementById('hmPhone').value.trim();
                const cityId = document.getElementById('hmCity').value;
                const cityOption = document.getElementById('hmCity').selectedOptions[0];
                const city = cityOption ? cityOption.textContent.trim() : '';
                const address = document.getElementById('hmAddress').value.trim();
                const size = document.getElementById('hmSelectedSize').value;
                const color = document.getElementById('hmSelectedColor').value;
                const colorId = document.getElementById('hmSelectedColorId').value;
                const selectedProduct = window.HM_SELECTED_PRODUCT || null;
                const productName = selectedProduct?.name || document.getElementById('hmProductName').textContent || 'منتج';
                const supplierProductId = selectedProduct?.supplier_product_id || hmNumericId(selectedProduct?.id || '');
                const quantity = document.getElementById('hmQuantity').value;
                const notes = document.getElementById('hmNotes').value;
                
                const missingFields = [];
                if (!selectedProduct || !supplierProductId) missingFields.push('المنتج');
                if (selectedProduct?.has_size_options && !size) missingFields.push('المقاس');
                if (selectedProduct?.has_color_options && !color) missingFields.push('اللون');
                if (name.length < 2) missingFields.push('الاسم الكامل (اسم واحد واضح مقبول، والاسم والنسب أفضل)');
                if (!(/^(?:0[67]\d{8}|212[67]\d{8})$/.test(phone.replace(/\D/g, ''))) || /^(?:0[67]|212[67])(\d)\1{7}$/.test(phone.replace(/\D/g, ''))) missingFields.push('رقم هاتف مغربي صحيح (06/07 أو 212)');
                if (!cityId) missingFields.push('المدينة');
                if (address.length < 10) missingFields.push('عنوان واضح للتوصيل حتى الباب');

                // التحقق من الحقول المطلوبة
                if (missingFields.length) {
                    try { window.hmTrackJourney?.('checkout_step', { journey_step: 'checkout_validation_failed', missing_fields: missingFields.join(' | '), product_id: selectedProduct?.id || '', product_name: productName, supplier: selectedProduct?.supplier || '' }); } catch (_) {}
                    messageDiv.style.display = 'block';
                    messageDiv.style.background = '#fff0f0';
                    messageDiv.style.color = '#b00020';
                    messageDiv.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-exclamation-circle"></i> باقي خاصك: ${missingFields.join('، ')}.`;
                    hmUpdateOrderAssistant();
                    return;
                }

                // تجهيز بيانات الطلب بالشكل الذي يقبله Webhook
                const metaEventId = hmCreateEventId('purchase');
                try { window.hmTrackJourney?.('checkout_step', { journey_step: 'checkout_submit_attempt', product_id: selectedProduct?.id || '', product_name: productName, supplier: selectedProduct?.supplier || '', quantity: parseInt(quantity) || 1, city }); } catch (_) {}

                const orderData = {
                    event_id: metaEventId,
                    meta_event_id: metaEventId,
                    tracking: visitorTracking,
                    session_id: visitorTracking.session_id,
                    landing_page: visitorTracking.landing_page,
                    referrer: visitorTracking.referrer,
                    utm_source: visitorTracking.utm_source,
                    utm_medium: visitorTracking.utm_medium,
                    utm_campaign: visitorTracking.utm_campaign,
                    utm_content: visitorTracking.utm_content,
                    utm_term: visitorTracking.utm_term,
                    fbclid: visitorTracking.fbclid,
                    ...hmGetAttribution(),
                    productName: productName,
                    product_name: productName,
                    productId: selectedProduct.product_id || `PRODUCT-${supplierProductId}`,
                    product_id: selectedProduct.product_id || `PRODUCT-${supplierProductId}`,
                    supplierProductId: supplierProductId,
                    supplier_product_id: supplierProductId,
                    size: size,
                    color: color,
                    colorId: colorId || '',
                    color_id: colorId || '',
                    variant_quantity: window.HM_SELECTED_COLOR?.quantity ?? null,
                    color_hex: (window.HM_SELECTED_COLOR && window.HM_SELECTED_COLOR.hex) || '',
                    quantity: parseInt(quantity) || 1,
                    customerName: name,
                    customer_name: name,
                    phone: phone,
                    delivery_phone: phone,
                    cityId: cityId,
                    city_id: cityId,
                    ville: cityId,
                    city: city,
                    address: address,
                    notes: notes || '',
                    pageUrl: window.location.href,
                    page_url: window.location.href,
                    product_price: selectedProduct?.price || '',
                    original_price: selectedProduct?.original_price || selectedProduct?.compare_at_price || '',
                    subtotal: (Number(selectedProduct?.price || 0) * (parseInt(quantity) || 1)) || '',
                    subtotal_price: (Number(selectedProduct?.price || 0) * (parseInt(quantity) || 1)) || '',
                    discount_amount: 0,
                    discount_type: '',
                    total_price: (Number(selectedProduct?.price || 0) * (parseInt(quantity) || 1)) || '',
                    final_total: (Number(selectedProduct?.price || 0) * (parseInt(quantity) || 1)) || '',
                    shipping_fee: 0,
                    delivery_fee: 0,
                    free_shipping: true,
                    price_includes_shipping: true,
                    profit_per_item: Number(selectedProduct?.profit_per_item || 20),
                    shipping_cost: Number(selectedProduct?.shipping_cost || 37),
                    shipping_included: selectedProduct?.shipping_included !== false,
                    delivery_included: selectedProduct?.delivery_included !== false,
                    pricing_rule: selectedProduct?.pricing_rule || 'supplier_price_plus_37_delivery_plus_20_profit',
                    supplier: selectedProduct?.supplier || '',
                    category: selectedProduct?.category_ar || selectedProduct?.category || '',
                    category_slug: selectedProduct?.category_slug || '',
                    product_category: selectedProduct?.product_category || selectedProduct?.category_ar || selectedProduct?.category || '',
                    product_category_slug: selectedProduct?.product_category_slug || selectedProduct?.category_slug || '',
                    display_category: selectedProduct?.display_category_ar || selectedProduct?.display_category || '',
                    display_category_slug: selectedProduct?.display_category_slug || '',
                    is_affilo: selectedProduct?.supplier === 'affilo',
                    product_image: (window.HM_SELECTED_COLOR && window.HM_SELECTED_COLOR.image) || selectedProduct?.main_image || selectedProduct?.primary_image_url || '',
                    product_source_url: selectedProduct?.source_url || '',
                    is_manual: Boolean(selectedProduct?.is_manual || selectedProduct?.supplier === 'manual'),
                    manual_product: selectedProduct?.is_manual || selectedProduct?.supplier === 'manual' ? selectedProduct : null,
                    live_product: selectedProduct,
                    source: 'hijab-makka-web'
                };
                Object.assign(orderData, window.hmEnhanceOrderData?.(orderData) || {});

                // عرض رسالة جاري الإرسال
                messageDiv.style.display = 'block';
                messageDiv.style.background = '#e8f0fe';
                messageDiv.style.color = '#1a56db';
                messageDiv.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-pulse"></i> جاري إرسال الطلب...';
                
                // تعطيل الزر لمنع الضغط المتكرر
                const submitBtn = document.getElementById('hmSubmit');
                submitBtn.disabled = true;
                submitBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-pulse"></i> جاري الإرسال...';

                try {

                    // التحقق من الكوبون قبل الإرسال، واستعماله لا يتم إلا بعد نجاح الطلب كاملاً.
                    let couponDiscount = 0;
                    let couponServiceNote = '';
                    if (orderData.coupon_code && HM_COUPON_VALIDATE_URL) {
                        let couponResponse = null;
                        let couponResult = {};
                        try {
                            couponResponse = await fetch(HM_COUPON_VALIDATE_URL, {
                                method: 'POST',
                                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Accept': 'application/json' },
                                body: JSON.stringify({ coupon_code: orderData.coupon_code, phone: orderData.phone })
                            });
                            couponResult = await couponResponse.json().catch(() => ({}));
                        } catch (couponError) {
                            couponServiceNote = 'تعذر الاتصال بخدمة الكوبون، تم تسجيل الطلب بدون تخفيض الكوبون وسنتحقق منه يدوياً.';
                        }
                        if (couponResponse && couponResult.service_unavailable === true) {
                            couponServiceNote = couponResult.message || 'خدمة الكوبون غير متوفرة مؤقتاً، تم تسجيل الطلب بدون تخفيض الكوبون وسنتحقق منه يدوياً.';
                        } else if (couponResponse && couponResponse.ok && couponResult.valid === true) {
                            couponDiscount = Number(couponResult.discount_amount || 20);
                            window.HM_VALIDATED_COUPON_DISCOUNT = couponDiscount;
                        } else if (couponResponse && couponResponse.status < 500) {
                            throw new Error(couponResult.message || 'رقم الطلب السابق غير صالح أو سبق استعماله.');
                        } else if (couponResponse) {
                            couponServiceNote = 'خدمة الكوبون غير متوفرة مؤقتاً، تم تسجيل الطلب بدون تخفيض الكوبون وسنتحقق منه يدوياً.';
                        }
                    }
                    
                    // إرسال كل منتج كطلب مستقل وبالتتابع. المورد لا يستقبل منتجين مختلفين في طلب واحد.
                    const cartItems = Array.isArray(orderData.items) && orderData.items.length
                        ? orderData.items
                        : [{
                            product_id: orderData.product_id,
                            supplier_product_id: orderData.supplier_product_id,
                            supplier: orderData.supplier,
                            name: orderData.product_name,
                            size: orderData.size,
                            color: orderData.color,
                            quantity: orderData.quantity,
                            unit_price: Number(orderData.product_price || 0),
                            subtotal: Number(orderData.subtotal_price || orderData.total_price || 0),
                            image: orderData.product_image || ''
                        }];
                    const cartSuppliers = [...new Set(cartItems.map(item => String(item.supplier || '').toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean))];
                    if (cartSuppliers.length > 1) throw new Error('لا يمكن إرسال منتجات من موردين مختلفين في نفس الطلب.');
                    const parentOrderId = `HMC-${Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase()}`;
                    const results = [];
                    let submittedTotal = 0;
                    for (let index = 0; index < cartItems.length; index += 1) {
                        const item = cartItems[index];
                        const allocatedDiscount = index === cartItems.length - 1 ? Number(orderData.discount_amount || 0) + couponDiscount : 0;
                        const itemSubtotal = Number(item.subtotal || (Number(item.unit_price || 0) * Number(item.quantity || 1)));
                        const itemData = {
                            ...orderData,
                            parent_order_id: parentOrderId,
                            cart_sequence: index + 1,
                            cart_items_count: cartItems.length,
                            items: [item],
                            cart_items: [item],
                            products_count: 1,
                            productName: item.name,
                            product_name: item.name,
                            productId: item.product_id,
                            product_id: item.product_id,
                            supplierProductId: item.supplier_product_id,
                            supplier_product_id: item.supplier_product_id,
                            supplier: item.supplier,
                            size: item.size,
                            color: item.color,
                            colorId: item.color_id || '',
                            color_id: item.color_id || '',
                            quantity: Number(item.quantity || 1),
                            product_price: Number(item.unit_price || 0),
                            subtotal_price: itemSubtotal,
                            discount_amount: allocatedDiscount,
                            subtotal: itemSubtotal,
                            discount_type: allocatedDiscount ? (couponDiscount ? 'coupon_or_bundle' : 'same_supplier_each_extra_item_37') : '',
                            profit_per_item: Number(item.profit_per_item || orderData.profit_per_item || 20),
                            shipping_cost: Number(item.shipping_cost || orderData.shipping_cost || 37),
                            pricing_rule: item.pricing_rule || orderData.pricing_rule || 'supplier_price_plus_37_delivery_plus_20_profit',
                            total_price: Math.max(0, itemSubtotal - allocatedDiscount),
                            final_total: Math.max(0, itemSubtotal - allocatedDiscount),
                            idempotency_key: `${parentOrderId}:${index + 1}:${item.supplier || ''}:${item.supplier_product_id || item.product_id || ''}`,
                            product_image: item.image || orderData.product_image || '',
                            notes: [orderData.notes, couponServiceNote, `طلب متعدد ${parentOrderId} — القطعة ${index + 1}/${cartItems.length}`, allocatedDiscount ? `تخفيض مجمع ${allocatedDiscount} د.م.` : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' | ')
                        };
                        const itemResponse = await fetch(WEBHOOK_URL, {
                            method: 'POST',
                            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Accept': 'application/json' },
                            body: JSON.stringify(itemData)
                        });
                        const itemResult = await itemResponse.clone().json().catch(() => ({}));
                        if (!itemResponse.ok || itemResult.success !== true) {
                            const error = new Error(itemResult.message || `فشل إرسال المنتج ${index + 1}`);
                            error.partial_results = results;
                            throw error;
                        }
                        results.push(itemResult);
                        submittedTotal += Number(itemData.final_total || 0);
                    }
                    const response = { ok: results.length === cartItems.length };
                    const result = {
                        success: response.ok,
                        order_id: results.map(item => item.order_id).filter(Boolean).join(' + '),
                        order_ids: results.map(item => item.order_id).filter(Boolean),
                        parent_order_id: parentOrderId,
                        status: 'pending_admin'
                    };
                    if (orderData.coupon_code && HM_COUPON_REDEEM_URL) {
                        const redeemResponse = await fetch(HM_COUPON_REDEEM_URL, {
                            method: 'POST',
                            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Accept': 'application/json' },
                            body: JSON.stringify({ coupon_code: orderData.coupon_code, phone: orderData.phone, order_id: result.order_id, parent_order_id: parentOrderId })
                        });
                        const redeemResult = await redeemResponse.json().catch(() => ({}));
                        if (!redeemResponse.ok || redeemResult.valid !== true) {
                        }
                    }

                    // التحقق من نجاح الإرسال (بدون الاعتماد على success)
                    if (response.ok && result.success === true) {
                        const purchasedContentIds = cartItems.map(item => String(item.product_id || item.supplier_product_id || '')).filter(Boolean);
                        const purchasedQuantity = cartItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + Number(item.quantity || 1), 0);
