    modal.querySelectorAll('[data-comments-close]').forEach(el=>el.addEventListener('click',close));
    document.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{if(event.key==='Escape'&&!modal.hidden)close();});
    window.addEventListener('pagehide',()=>clearInterval(autoTimer),{once:true});
  }

  function initFaqGuides(){qa('.hm-faq-guide').forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{const target=q(btn.dataset.guideTarget);if(!target)return;target.scrollIntoView({behavior:reduce?'auto':'smooth',block:'center'});target.classList.remove('hm-guide-highlight');void target.offsetWidth;target.classList.add('hm-guide-highlight');const first=target.querySelector('button,input,select');setTimeout(()=>first?.focus(),reduce?0:450);}));}

  function initSuccessPanel(){const message=q('#hmMessage'),panel=q('#hmSuccessPanel'),button=q('#hmSuccessInvoiceButton');if(!message||!panel)return;const show=()=>{if(/تسجيل طلبك بنجاح|تم تسجيل طلبك/.test(message.textContent)){panel.hidden=false;panel.scrollIntoView({behavior:reduce?'auto':'smooth',block:'center'});}};new MutationObserver(show).observe(message,{childList:true,subtree:true,characterData:true});button?.addEventListener('click',()=>{const invoice=q('#hmLiveSummary .hm-invoice-card');invoice?.scrollIntoView({behavior:reduce?'auto':'smooth',block:'center'});invoice?.classList.add('hm-guide-highlight');setTimeout(()=>invoice?.classList.remove('hm-guide-highlight'),1700);});}

  function overrideCatalogFilter(){
    window.hmApplyCatalogFilter=function(filter='all'){
      const query=String(q('#hmProductSearch')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();const cards=qa('#hmLiveProductsGrid .product-card');let visible=0;
      qa('.hm-filter').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.filter===filter));qa('.hm-category-card').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-selected',String(b.dataset.category===filter)));
      cards.forEach((card,index)=>{let match=true;if(filter==='new')match=index<6;else if(filter==='3')match=card.dataset.pieces==='3';else if(filter==='4')match=card.dataset.pieces==='4';else if(filter==='plus')match=card.dataset.plus==='true';else if(filter!=='all')match=card.dataset.category===filter;const search=!query||String(card.dataset.search||card.textContent).toLowerCase().includes(query);const show=match&&search;card.hidden=!show;if(show)visible++;});
      const count=q('#hmVisibleCount');if(count)count.textContent=String(visible);const results=q('#hmSearchResults');if(results)results.textContent=visible?`${visible} نتيجة`:'ما لقينا حتى منتج بهاد البحث.';
      try{
        const url=new URL(location.href);
        [...url.searchParams.keys()].forEach(key=>{if(/product$/i.test(key))url.searchParams.delete(key);});
        url.searchParams.delete('product');
        if(filter==='all')url.searchParams.delete('category');
        else if(!['new','3','4','plus'].includes(filter))url.searchParams.set('category',filter);
        history.replaceState(null,'',url);
      }catch(_){}
    };
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{
    overrideCatalogFilter();initHeroCarousel();initSelectWrapper();initQuantity();initSimilarToggle();initComments();initFaqGuides();initSuccessPanel();
    const products=q('#hmProductsList');if(products)new MutationObserver(()=>{setTimeout(()=>{overrideCatalogFilter();syncSelectedProduct();},0);}).observe(products,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  });
})();
</script>
<script id="hmWhatsAppNewsletterScript">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const WHATSAPP_NUMBER='212660005342';
  const form=document.getElementById('hmNewsletterForm');
  const select=document.getElementById('hmNewsletterInterest');
  const nameInput=document.getElementById('hmNewsletterName');
  const status=document.getElementById('hmNewsletterStatus');
  if(!form||!select)return;

  function clean(value){return String(value??'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();}
  function populateInterests(){
    const products=Array.isArray(window.HM_PRODUCTS)?window.HM_PRODUCTS:[];
    if(!products.length)return false;
    const current=select.value;
    const categories=new Map();
    products.forEach(product=>{
      const slug=clean(product.category_slug||product.category_ar||product.category);
      const label=clean(product.category_ar||product.category)||'منتجات أخرى';
      if(slug&&!categories.has(slug))categories.set(slug,label);
    });
    select.innerHTML='<option value="جميع المنتجات">جميع المنتجات</option>'+[...categories.values()].map(label=>`<option value="${label.replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}">${label.replace(/</g,'&lt;')}</option>`).join('');
    if([...select.options].some(option=>option.value===current))select.value=current;
    return true;
  }

  form.addEventListener('submit',event=>{
    event.preventDefault();
    const name=clean(nameInput?.value);
    const interest=clean(select.value)||'جميع المنتجات';
    const lines=[
      'السلام عليكم، بغيت نشترك في نشرة HIJAB MAKKAH عبر واتساب.',
      name?`الاسم: ${name}`:'',
      `الاهتمام: ${interest}`,
      'موافقة: بغيت نوصل بالجديد، رجوع المخزون والعروض المهمة، ونقدر نوقف الاشتراك فأي وقت.'
    ].filter(Boolean);
    const url=`https://wa.me/${WHATSAPP_NUMBER}?text=${encodeURIComponent(lines.join('\n'))}`;
    if(status)status.textContent='واتساب غادي يتحل دابا؛ غير اضغطي على إرسال باش يكتمل الاشتراك.';
    try{window.HMTrack?.event?.('newsletter_whatsapp_click',{interest});}catch(_){}
    try{window.hmTrackMeta?.('Lead',{content_name:'WhatsApp Newsletter',content_category:interest});}catch(_){}
    const opened=window.open(url,'_blank','noopener');
    if(!opened)window.location.href=url;
  });

  if(!populateInterests()){
    const poll=setInterval(()=>{if(populateInterests())clearInterval(poll);},350);
    setTimeout(()=>clearInterval(poll),20000);
  }
})();
</script>

  function buildTrackingModal(){

    modal.innerHTML = `

          <a href="#hmOrder" id="hmTrackingNewOrder"><i class="fas fa-bag-shopping"></i> طلب منتج جديد</a>
        </div>
        <div class="hm-tracking-result" id="hmInlineTrackingResult">

  function openTrackingModal(){
    const modal=document.getElementById('hmTrackingPage');
    if(!modal)return;
    modal.hidden=false;
    document.body.classList.add('hm-tracking-open');
    setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('hmInlineOrderId')?.focus(),40);
    try{window.HMTrack?.event?.('order_tracking_open',{});}catch(_){ }
  }
  window.hmOpenTrackingPage = openTrackingModal;

  function closeTrackingModal(){
    const modal=document.getElementById('hmTrackingPage');
    if(!modal)return;
    modal.hidden=true;
    document.body.classList.remove('hm-tracking-open');
    if(location.hash==='#hmTrackingPage')history.replaceState(null,'',location.pathname+location.search);
  }

  function addNavLinks(){
    const desktopNav = document.querySelector('.hm-header-nav');
    if(desktopNav && !desktopNav.querySelector('.hm-track-nav-link')){
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = '#hmTrackingPage';
      link.className = 'hm-track-nav-link';
      link.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-truck-fast"></i><span>التتبع</span>';
      desktopNav.appendChild(link);
    }
    const mobileNav = document.querySelector('.hm-header-mobile-nav');
    if(mobileNav && !mobileNav.querySelector('.hm-track-nav-link')){
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = '#hmTrackingPage';
      link.className = 'hm-track-nav-link';
      link.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-truck-fast"></i><span>التتبع</span>';
      mobileNav.appendChild(link);
    }
    document.querySelectorAll('.hm-track-nav-link').forEach(link=>link.addEventListener('click',event=>{
      event.preventDefault();
      openTrackingModal();
    }));
  }

  function initTrackingModal(){
    const modal=document.getElementById('hmTrackingPage');
    if(!modal)return;
    modal.querySelectorAll('[data-tracking-close]').forEach(el=>el.addEventListener('click',closeTrackingModal));
    document.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{if(event.key==='Escape'&&!modal.hidden)closeTrackingModal();});
    document.getElementById('hmTrackingNewOrder')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      closeTrackingModal();
      setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('hmOrder')?.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'}),50);
    });
  }

  function initTrackingForm(){
    const form = document.getElementById('hmInlineTrackingForm');
    const orderInput = document.getElementById('hmInlineOrderId');
    const phoneInput = document.getElementById('hmInlinePhone');
    const alertBox = document.getElementById('hmInlineTrackingAlert');
    const result = document.getElementById('hmInlineTrackingResult');
    const frame = document.getElementById('hmInlineTrackingFrame');
    const whatsapp = document.getElementById('hmInlineTrackingWhatsapp');
    if(!form || !orderInput || !phoneInput || !alertBox || !result || !frame) return;

    const showAlert = message => {
      alertBox.textContent = message;
      alertBox.style.display = 'block';
    };
    const clearAlert = () => {
      alertBox.textContent = '';
      alertBox.style.display = 'none';
    };
    const mark = () => {
      const orderOk = orderInput.value.trim().length >= 6;
      const phoneOk = isMoroccanMobile(phoneInput.value);
      orderInput.classList.toggle('hm-valid', orderOk);
      orderInput.classList.toggle('hm-invalid', Boolean(orderInput.value.trim()) && !orderOk);
      phoneInput.classList.toggle('hm-valid', phoneOk);
      phoneInput.classList.toggle('hm-invalid', Boolean(phoneInput.value.trim()) && !phoneOk);
    };

    orderInput.addEventListener('input', mark);
    phoneInput.addEventListener('input', mark);

    form.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
      event.preventDefault();
      clearAlert();
      const orderId = orderInput.value.trim();
      const phone = normalizePhone(phoneInput.value);
      if(orderId.length < 6){
        showAlert('كتبي رقم الطلب كامل كما ظهر لك بعد تسجيل الطلب.');
        orderInput.focus();
        mark();
        return;
      }
      if(!isMoroccanMobile(phone)){
        showAlert('رقم الهاتف خاصو يكون مغربي صحيح: يبدأ بـ 06 أو 07 ويتكون من 10 أرقام.');
        phoneInput.focus();
        mark();
        return;
      }
      const url = TRACKING_ENDPOINT + '?order_id=' + encodeURIComponent(orderId) + '&phone=' + encodeURIComponent(phone);
      if(whatsapp){
        whatsapp.href = 'https://wa.me/' + SUPPORT_WHATSAPP + '?text=' + encodeURIComponent('سلام، بغيت نتبع طلبي. رقم الطلب: ' + orderId + '، الهاتف: ' + phone);
      }
      try {
        showAlert('كنقلبو على الطلب ديالك...');
        const response = await fetch(url, { method: 'GET', cache: 'no-store' });
        const contentType = response.headers.get('content-type') || '';
        if (!response.ok || contentType.includes('application/json')) {
          let message = 'ما قدرناش نلقاو حالة الطلب دابا. تأكدي من رقم الطلب والهاتف، أو تواصلي معنا في واتساب.';
          try {
            const data = await response.clone().json();
            if (data?.message && !String(data.message).includes('webhook')) message = data.message;
          } catch (_) {}
          result.style.display = 'none';
          showAlert(message);
          return;
        }
        clearAlert();
        frame.src = url;
        result.style.display = 'block';
        result.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'nearest'});
        try{ window.HMTrack?.event?.('order_tracking_search',{order_id:orderId}); }catch(_){ }
      } catch (_) {
        result.style.display = 'none';
        showAlert('وقع مشكل مؤقت في التتبع. جربي مرة أخرى أو تواصلي معنا في واتساب.');
      }
    });
    mark();
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function(){
    injectTrackingStyles();
    buildTrackingModal();
    addNavLinks();
    initTrackingModal();
    initTrackingForm();
    if(location.hash === '#hmTrackingPage') setTimeout(openTrackingModal,180);
  });
})();
</script>
<script id="hmConversionUpgradeScript">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const q=(s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);
  const qa=(s,r=document)=>Array.from(r.querySelectorAll(s));
  const clean=v=>String(v??'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
  const esc=v=>clean(v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
  const reduce=window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)')?.matches;
  let lastDraft={};
  let shownOrderId='';

  function canonical(value){
    const raw=clean(value).toLowerCase();
    if(/burkini|borkini|bourkini|بوركيني/.test(raw))return 'burkini';
    if(/access|اكسسوار|إكسسوار/.test(raw))return 'accessories';
    return raw.replace(/[^a-z0-9\u0600-\u06ff]+/g,'-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g,'');
  }

  function readRoute(){
    const params=new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    let category=clean(params.get('category'));
    let productId=clean(params.get('product'));
    let productKey='';
    for(const [key,value] of params.entries()){
      const k=clean(key);
      const match=k.match(/^(.+?)product$/i);
      if(match){productKey=k;if(!category)category=match[1];if(!productId&&clean(value))productId=clean(value);continue;}
      if(!category&&!clean(value)&&/burkini|borkini|bourkini|بوركيني|access|اكسسوار|إكسسوار/i.test(k))category=k;
    }
    return {category:category?canonical(category):'all',productId,productKey,type:productId?'product':(category?'category':'store')};
  }
  window.hmSmartLandingRoute=readRoute;

  function categoryLabel(slug){
    const products=Array.isArray(window.HM_PRODUCTS)?window.HM_PRODUCTS:[];
    const hit=products.find(p=>canonical(p.category_slug||p.category_ar||p.category)===canonical(slug));
    if(hit)return clean(hit.category_ar||hit.category)||slug;
    if(canonical(slug)==='burkini')return 'البوركيني';
    if(canonical(slug)==='accessories')return 'الإكسسوارات';
    return slug;
  }

  function categoryCount(slug){
    return (Array.isArray(window.HM_PRODUCTS)?window.HM_PRODUCTS:[]).filter(p=>canonical(p.category_slug||p.category_ar||p.category)===canonical(slug)).length;
  }

  function showDeepLinkBanner(){
    const route=readRoute();
    if(route.type!=='category'||route.category==='all')return;
    const host=q('#hmModelsSection .container');
    const anchor=q('#hmModelsSection .hm-catalog-head');
    if(!host||!anchor)return;
    let banner=q('#hmDeepLinkBanner');
    if(!banner){
      banner=document.createElement('aside');
      banner.id='hmDeepLinkBanner';
      banner.className='hm-deeplink-banner';
      anchor.parentNode.insertBefore(banner,anchor);
    }
    const label=categoryLabel(route.category);
    const count=categoryCount(route.category);
    banner.innerHTML=`<div class="hm-deeplink-copy"><i class="fas fa-arrow-turn-down"></i><div><span>وصلتي مباشرة من رابط التصنيف</span><strong>${esc(label)}</strong><small>${count?`${count} منتج متوفر دابا`:'كنحمّلو منتجات هذا التصنيف'}</small></div></div><button class="hm-deeplink-all" type="button"><i class="fas fa-layer-group"></i> جميع التصنيفات</button>`;
    banner.hidden=false;
    banner.querySelector('button')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      try{
        const url=new URL(location.href);
        url.searchParams.delete('category');
        [...url.searchParams.keys()].forEach(key=>{if(/product$/i.test(key))url.searchParams.delete(key);});
        history.replaceState(null,'',url);
      }catch(_){ }
      window.hmApplyCatalogFilter?.('all');
      banner.hidden=true;
    },{once:true});
    setTimeout(()=>q('#hmModelsSection')?.scrollIntoView({behavior:reduce?'auto':'smooth',block:'start'}),180);
    try{window.HMTrack?.event?.('smart_category_landing',{category_slug:route.category,visible_products:count,utm_source:new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('utm_source')||''});}catch(_){ }
  }

  function findProduct(id){return (Array.isArray(window.HM_PRODUCTS)?window.HM_PRODUCTS:[]).find(p=>clean(p.id||p.product_id).toLowerCase()===clean(id).toLowerCase());}
  function imageList(product){return [...new Set([product?.main_image,product?.primary_image_url,...(Array.isArray(product?.images)?product.images:[])].filter(Boolean))].slice(0,10);}
  function colors(product){return (Array.isArray(product?.all_colors)&&product.all_colors.length?product.all_colors:product?.colors)||[];}
  function sizes(product){return (Array.isArray(product?.all_sizes)&&product.all_sizes.length?product.all_sizes:product?.sizes)||[];}
  function colorLabel(color){return clean(typeof color==='string'?color:(color?.label||color?.name||color?.color_name));}
  function colorHex(color){
    if(color&&typeof color==='object'&&color.hex)return color.hex;
    const name=colorLabel(color).toLowerCase();
    const map={noir:'#171512',black:'#171512',أسود:'#171512',blanc:'#f5f3ee',white:'#f5f3ee',أبيض:'#f5f3ee',beige:'#d7b98e',بيج:'#d7b98e',rose:'#d98a9b',وردي:'#d98a9b',bleu:'#527da8',أزرق:'#527da8',vert:'#5d8364',أخضر:'#5d8364',gris:'#8d9195',رمادي:'#8d9195',marron:'#795548',بني:'#795548'};
    for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(map))if(name.includes(key))return value;
    return '#d8cbc5';
  }

  function renderSmartQuick(product){
    const modal=q('#hmQuickView');const body=q('#hmQuickBody');if(!modal||!body||!product)return;
    const imgs=imageList(product);const first=imgs[0]||'';
    const sizeHtml=sizes(product).filter(Boolean).slice(0,12).map(size=>`<span class="hm-smart-pill"><i class="fas fa-ruler"></i>${esc(size)}</span>`).join('')||'<span class="hm-smart-pill">التوفر يظهر عند الاختيار</span>';
    const colorHtml=colors(product).filter(Boolean).slice(0,12).map(color=>`<span class="hm-smart-pill"><i class="hm-smart-swatch" style="background:${esc(colorHex(color))}"></i>${esc(colorLabel(color)||'لون متوفر')}</span>`).join('')||'<span class="hm-smart-pill">الألوان تظهر حسب المخزون</span>';
    body.innerHTML=`<div class="hm-smart-quick"><div class="hm-smart-gallery"><img id="hmSmartMainImage" class="hm-smart-main" src="${esc(first)}" alt="${esc(product.name)}"><div class="hm-smart-thumbs">${imgs.map((img,index)=>`<button class="hm-smart-thumb" type="button" aria-pressed="${index===0?'true':'false'}" data-image="${esc(img)}"><img src="${esc(img)}" alt="صورة ${index+1} من ${esc(product.name)}"></button>`).join('')}</div></div><div class="hm-smart-copy"><div class="hm-smart-meta"><span class="hm-smart-category"><i class="fas fa-layer-group"></i>${esc(product.category_ar||product.category||'منتج مختار')}</span><span class="hm-stock is-in">متوفر</span></div><h2>${esc(product.name)}</h2><div class="hm-smart-code">كود المنتج: ${esc(product.id||product.product_id||'—')}</div><div class="hm-smart-price">${Number(product.price||0)} د.م.</div><p class="hm-smart-description">${esc(product.description||'شاهدي الصور والمقاسات والألوان المتوفرة، ومن بعد اختاري الموديل لإكمال الطلب.')}</p><div class="hm-smart-block"><b><i class="fas fa-ruler-combined"></i> المقاسات المتوفرة</b><div class="hm-smart-pills">${sizeHtml}</div></div><div class="hm-smart-block"><b><i class="fas fa-palette"></i> الألوان المتوفرة</b><div class="hm-smart-pills">${colorHtml}</div></div><div class="hm-smart-actions"><button class="hm-smart-select" id="hmSmartChoose" type="button"><i class="fas fa-bag-shopping"></i> اختاري هذا الموديل</button><button class="hm-smart-details" id="hmSmartDetails" type="button"><i class="fas fa-images"></i> التفاصيل الكاملة</button></div></div></div>`;
    modal.hidden=false;
    qa('.hm-smart-thumb',body).forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{q('#hmSmartMainImage').src=btn.dataset.image;qa('.hm-smart-thumb',body).forEach(x=>x.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(x===btn)));}));
    const choose=()=>{modal.hidden=true;window.selectProduct?.(product.id||product.product_id);};
    q('#hmSmartChoose')?.addEventListener('click',choose);
    q('#hmSmartDetails')?.addEventListener('click',choose);
    setTimeout(()=>q('.hm-modal-close',modal)?.focus(),20);
    try{window.HMTrack?.event?.('smart_quick_view',{product_id:product.id||product.product_id,category_slug:product.category_slug||''});}catch(_){ }
  }

  function initSmartQuickView(){
    document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
      const button=event.target.closest('.hm-quick-btn');
      if(!button)return;
      const card=button.closest('.product-card');
      const product=findProduct(card?.dataset.id);
      if(!product)return;
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
      event.stopImmediatePropagation();
      const landingUrl=window.hmProductLandingUrl?.(product);
      if(landingUrl)window.location.assign(landingUrl);
    },true);
  }

  function snapshotOrder(){
    const city=q('#hmCity');
    lastDraft={
      name:clean(q('#hmName')?.value),phone:clean(q('#hmPhone')?.value),city:clean(city?.selectedOptions?.[0]?.textContent),address:clean(q('#hmAddress')?.value),notes:clean(q('#hmNotes')?.value),capturedAt:new Date().toISOString()
    };
  }

  function ensureConfirmation(){
    let modal=q('#hmConfirmationPage');if(modal)return modal;
    modal=document.createElement('section');modal.id='hmConfirmationPage';modal.className='hm-confirm-page';modal.hidden=true;modal.setAttribute('role','dialog');modal.setAttribute('aria-modal','true');modal.setAttribute('aria-labelledby','hmConfirmationTitle');
    modal.innerHTML='<div class="hm-confirm-backdrop" data-confirm-close></div><article class="hm-confirm-card"><button class="hm-confirm-close" type="button" data-confirm-close aria-label="إغلاق"><i class="fas fa-xmark"></i></button><div id="hmConfirmationBody"></div></article>';
    document.body.appendChild(modal);
    modal.querySelectorAll('[data-confirm-close]').forEach(el=>el.addEventListener('click',closeConfirmation));
    return modal;
  }

  function closeConfirmation(){const modal=q('#hmConfirmationPage');if(modal)modal.hidden=true;document.body.classList.remove('hm-confirm-open');}

  function openTracking(orderId,phone){
    closeConfirmation();
    const tracking=q('#hmTrackingPage');
    if(!tracking){location.hash='hmTrackingPage';return;}
    tracking.hidden=false;document.body.classList.add('hm-tracking-open');
    const order=q('#hmInlineOrderId'),tel=q('#hmInlinePhone');if(order)order.value=orderId;if(tel)tel.value=phone;
    order?.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));tel?.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));
    setTimeout(()=>q('#hmInlineTrackingForm button[type="submit"]')?.focus(),40);
  }

  function confirmationData(orderId){
    const invoice=window.HM_INVOICE_DATA||{};
    const product=invoice.product||window.HM_SELECTED_PRODUCT||{};
    const firstItem=Array.isArray(invoice.items)&&invoice.items.length?invoice.items[0]:null;
    const phone=lastDraft.phone||(()=>{try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('hm_last_order')||'{}').phone||'';}catch(_){return '';}})();
    return {orderId,phone,name:lastDraft.name||'العميلة',city:lastDraft.city||'—',address:lastDraft.address||'—',productName:firstItem?.name||product.name||'طلب HIJAB MAKKAH',productImage:firstItem?.image||invoice.imageUrl||product.main_image||product.primary_image_url||product.images?.[0]||'',size:firstItem?.size||invoice.size||'—',color:firstItem?.color||invoice.color||'—',quantity:invoice.quantity||firstItem?.quantity||1,total:invoice.total??window.HM_SELECTED_PRODUCT?.price??0};
  }

  function showConfirmation(orderId){
    if(!orderId||shownOrderId===orderId)return;shownOrderId=orderId;
    const modal=ensureConfirmation();const body=q('#hmConfirmationBody',modal);const data=confirmationData(orderId);
    body.innerHTML=`<div class="hm-confirm-head"><div class="hm-confirm-check"><i class="fas fa-check"></i></div><h2 id="hmConfirmationTitle">طلبك تسجل بنجاح</h2><p>احتافظي برقم الطلب. غادي نتواصلوا معك لتأكيد المنتج والعنوان قبل الشحن.</p><span class="hm-confirm-order-number"><span>رقم الطلب</span><b>${esc(data.orderId)}</b></span></div><div class="hm-confirm-product">${data.productImage?`<img src="${esc(data.productImage)}" alt="${esc(data.productName)}">`:'<div class="hm-confirm-image-placeholder"></div>'}<div><h3>${esc(data.productName)}</h3><div class="hm-confirm-tags"><span>المقاس: ${esc(data.size)}</span><span>اللون: ${esc(data.color)}</span><span>الكمية: ${esc(data.quantity)}</span></div><div class="hm-confirm-total">المجموع النهائي<strong>${Number(data.total||0)} د.م.</strong></div></div></div><div class="hm-confirm-details"><div class="hm-confirm-detail"><small>الاسم</small><b>${esc(data.name)}</b></div><div class="hm-confirm-detail"><small>الهاتف</small><b dir="ltr">${esc(data.phone)}</b></div><div class="hm-confirm-detail"><small>المدينة</small><b>${esc(data.city)}</b></div><div class="hm-confirm-detail"><small>العنوان</small><b>${esc(data.address)}</b></div></div><div class="hm-confirm-actions"><button class="hm-confirm-track" id="hmConfirmTrack" type="button"><i class="fas fa-truck-fast"></i> تتبع الطلب</button><button class="hm-confirm-invoice" id="hmConfirmInvoice" type="button"><i class="fas fa-download"></i> تحميل الفاتورة</button><button class="hm-confirm-shop" id="hmConfirmShop" type="button"><i class="fas fa-store"></i> متابعة التسوق</button></div>`;
    modal.hidden=false;document.body.classList.add('hm-confirm-open');
    q('#hmConfirmTrack')?.addEventListener('click',()=>openTracking(data.orderId,data.phone));
    q('#hmConfirmInvoice')?.addEventListener('click',()=>window.hmDownloadInvoiceImage?.());
    q('#hmConfirmShop')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{closeConfirmation();q('#hmModelsSection')?.scrollIntoView({behavior:reduce?'auto':'smooth',block:'start'});});
    setTimeout(()=>q('#hmConfirmTrack')?.focus(),30);
    try{window.HMTrack?.event?.('order_confirmation_view',{order_id:data.orderId,product_id:productIdFromInvoice()});}catch(_){ }
  }

  function productIdFromInvoice(){const invoice=window.HM_INVOICE_DATA||{};return invoice.items?.[0]?.product_id||invoice.product?.id||window.HM_SELECTED_PRODUCT?.id||'';}

  function observeSuccess(){
    const message=q('#hmMessage');if(!message)return;
    const inspect=()=>{
      const value=clean(message.textContent);
      if(!/تم تسجيل طلبك بنجاح|طلبك تسجل بنجاح/.test(value))return;
      const match=value.match(/رقم الطلب\s*:\s*([^\n]+?)(?=\s*تتبعي|\s*غادي|$)/i);
      let orderId=clean(match?.[1]);
      if(!orderId){try{orderId=clean(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('hm_last_order')||'{}').order_id);}catch(_){}}
      if(orderId)setTimeout(()=>showConfirmation(orderId),120);
    };
    new MutationObserver(inspect).observe(message,{childList:true,subtree:true,characterData:true});inspect();
  }

  function init(){
    initSmartQuickView();
    q('#hmOrderForm')?.addEventListener('submit',snapshotOrder,true);
    observeSuccess();ensureConfirmation();
    window.addEventListener('hm:products-ready',showDeepLinkBanner,{once:true});
    if(window.HM_PRODUCTS_READY)setTimeout(showDeepLinkBanner,80);
    document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'&&!q('#hmConfirmationPage')?.hidden)closeConfirmation();});
    const route=readRoute();
    try{sessionStorage.setItem('hm_smart_landing_route',JSON.stringify({...route,url:location.href,at:new Date().toISOString()}));}catch(_){ }
    if(route.type==='product')try{window.HMTrack?.event?.('smart_product_landing',{product_id:route.productId,category_slug:route.category,utm_source:new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('utm_source')||''});}catch(_){ }
  }
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init);
})();
</script>

<button id="hmBackToTop" type="button" aria-label="العودة إلى أعلى الصفحة" title="العودة للأعلى">
  <i class="fas fa-arrow-up" aria-hidden="true"></i>
</button>
<script id="hm-back-to-top-script">
(function(){
  const button=document.getElementById('hmBackToTop');
  if(!button)return;
  const update=()=>button.classList.toggle('is-visible',window.scrollY>420);
  button.addEventListener('click',()=>window.scrollTo({
    top:0,
    behavior:window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches?'auto':'smooth'
  }));
  window.addEventListener('scroll',update,{passive:true});
  update();
})();
</script>


<script id="hm-direct-product-landing-links">
(function(){
  "use strict";
  if(window.__HM_DIRECT_PRODUCT_LANDING_LINKS__)return;
  window.__HM_DIRECT_PRODUCT_LANDING_LINKS__=true;

  var LANDING_PATH="/pages/product-offer";

  function clean(value){
    return String(value==null?"":value).trim()
  }

  function productNumber(product){
    var source=clean(
      product.supplier_product_id||
      product.original_id||
      product.product_id||
      product.id
    );
    var matches=source.match(/\d+/g);
    return matches&&matches.length?matches[matches.length-1]:""
  }

  function slugify(value){
    return clean(value)
      .toLowerCase()
      .normalize("NFD")
      .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,"")
      .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}]+/gu,"-")
      .replace(/^-+|-+$/g,"")
      .replace(/-{2,}/g,"-")
  }

  function productSlug(product){
    var nameSlug=slugify(product.name||product.title||"product")||"product";
    var number=productNumber(product);
    return number?nameSlug+"-"+number:nameSlug
  }

  function landingUrl(product){
    var url=new URL(LANDING_PATH,window.location.origin);
    var current=new URL(window.location.href);

    [
      "utm_source","utm_medium","utm_campaign","utm_content","utm_term",
      "fbclid","gclid","ttclid","ad_id","adset_id","campaign_id"
    ].forEach(function(key){
      var value=current.searchParams.get(key);
      if(value)url.searchParams.set(key,value)
    });

    url.hash=encodeURIComponent(productSlug(product));
    return url.toString()
  }

  window.hmProductLandingUrl=landingUrl;

  function findProduct(card){
    var id=clean(card&&card.dataset&&card.dataset.id);
    if(!id||!Array.isArray(window.HM_PRODUCTS))return null;

    return window.HM_PRODUCTS.find(function(product){
      return clean(product.id)===id||clean(product.product_id)===id
    })||null
  }

  function configureCards(){
    document.querySelectorAll("#hmLiveProductsGrid .product-card").forEach(function(card){
      var product=findProduct(card);
      if(!product)return;

      card.setAttribute("aria-label","شوفي "+clean(product.name)+" داخل الموقع");

      var selectButton=card.querySelector(".hm-select-btn,[data-select-product]");
      if(selectButton&&!card.dataset.active){
        selectButton.innerHTML='<i class="fas fa-bag-shopping"></i><span>شوفي المنتج</span>'
      }
      if(selectButton){
        selectButton.setAttribute("aria-label","شوفي "+clean(product.name)+" داخل الموقع")
      }

      var eyeButton=card.querySelector(".hm-quick-btn");
      if(eyeButton){
        eyeButton.style.display="";
        eyeButton.title="فتح صفحة المنتج المستقلة";
        eyeButton.setAttribute("aria-label","فتح صفحة "+clean(product.name)+" المستقلة");
        eyeButton.onclick=function(event){
          event.preventDefault();
          event.stopPropagation();
          window.location.assign(landingUrl(product))
        }
      }

      var actions=card.querySelector(".hm-card-actions");
      if(actions)actions.style.gridTemplateColumns="minmax(0,1fr) 42px"
    })
  }

  window.addEventListener("hm:products-ready",function(){
    setTimeout(configureCards,0);
    setTimeout(configureCards,700)
  });

  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",function(){
    var grid=document.getElementById("hmProductsList");
    if(grid&&window.MutationObserver){
      new MutationObserver(function(){
        setTimeout(configureCards,0)
      }).observe(grid,{childList:true,subtree:true})
    }
    setTimeout(configureCards,1200)
  })
})();
</script>

</body>
</html>
